# Wire timeline drop to use the existing local-file-handle flow

Read CLAUDE.md at the repo root first — it has cross-cutting rules and points to relevant sub-docs.

## Problem

When a user drops a large file onto the **timeline**, it always uploads to Supabase Storage. It should instead use the existing local-file-handle flow (`storage_mode='local'` + `local_handle_id`, served via `useLocalMediaUrl()` with `LocalMediaPermissionOverlay` as the recovery UX) — the same way the gallery's `useDropToGeneration` already works for large files.

## What already exists (do NOT rebuild)

- **Local-handle write path**: `src/shared/lib/media/createGenerationFromFile.ts` — `createGenerationForLocalFile()` writes `storage_mode='local'` + `local_handle_id` and persists the handle via `saveHandle()` from `src/shared/lib/media/localHandleStore.ts`.
- **Threshold + browser-capability gate**: `src/features/gallery/hooks/useDropToGeneration.ts` checks file size against `IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES` from `src/shared/lib/media/dropToGenerationConfig.ts` AND that `DataTransferItem.getAsFileSystemHandle()` is available. Use the **same** gate.
- **Resolver + permission UX**: `useLocalMediaUrl()` in `src/shared/media/localMediaResolver.ts` and `LocalMediaPermissionOverlay.tsx` already handle rendering + recovery — no changes needed there.

## What's broken

The timeline drop pipeline is a separate, older path that never got updated:

- Drop handler chain ends at `useDropActions.handleTimelineImageDrop` → calls `processDroppedImages` in `src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.ts`.
- `processDroppedImages` only receives `File[]`. It calls `processSingleDroppedImage`, which **unconditionally** calls `createGenerationForUploadedImage`. No size check, no `getAsFileSystemHandle()` probe, no local branch.

There is also an intermediate chain of timeline drop hooks (in the travel-between-images tool) that strips/discards `DataTransferItem` info before it reaches `useDropActions`. Those hooks must be updated to capture and forward handles.

## The fix

1. **Capture the `FileSystemFileHandle` synchronously** at the timeline drop event boundary. The `DataTransferItem.getAsFileSystemHandle()` call MUST happen synchronously inside the drop handler (or its returned promises captured before any await), because `DataTransferItem` is invalidated after the event resolves. Look at how `useDropToGeneration` does this and replicate the pattern.

2. **Thread an optional `FileSystemFileHandle | null` array through** the timeline drop hooks → `useDropActions.handleTimelineImageDrop` → `ExternalImageDropVariables` → `processDroppedImages` → `processSingleDroppedImage`. The new array is parallel to `imageFiles`. Make it optional so non-timeline callers aren't broken.

3. **Branch in `processSingleDroppedImage`** using the same gate as `useDropToGeneration` — file size ≥ `IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES` AND a handle is present. When the gate passes, call `createGenerationForLocalFile`. Otherwise keep current behavior (`createGenerationForUploadedImage`).

4. **Crop interaction (important)**: `cropFilesIfNeeded` may return a *new* `File` for a given input. If it did, the captured handle no longer corresponds to the bytes you're storing — fall back to upload in that case. Use reference equality (`processedFile === originalFile`) to detect this.

5. **Don't break callers.** The new param is optional. Other callers of `processDroppedImages` (batch drop, app-level drop, shot-creation flows) keep current behavior unchanged because they don't supply handles.

## Constraints

- Don't refactor unrelated code. Don't add new abstractions. Don't add comments explaining the change.
- Reuse existing helpers from `useDropToGeneration` if possible (e.g. the `supportsLocalFileHandles()` probe pattern). Keep the gate condition identical to what the gallery uses so behavior stays consistent.
- The `local_handle_id` is a UUID generated client-side and is the link between the DB row and the IndexedDB-persisted handle. Mirror exactly how the gallery flow generates and stores it.

## Tests

- Mirror the patterns in `src/features/gallery/hooks/useDropToGeneration.test.tsx` and the existing `src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.test.ts` (if present).
- Add at least two cases on the `externalImageDrop` side: (a) large file + handle → local-handle path is taken, (b) small file or no handle → upload path is taken.

## Verification

- Run `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json`. Note: the codebase has pre-existing type errors. Your changes must not introduce *new* errors beyond the pre-existing pattern that already affects `useDropToGeneration.ts` (`FileSystemFileHandle` missing `queryPermission`/`requestPermission` lib types).
- Run vitest on the affected directories: `src/shared/hooks/shots`, `src/features/gallery/hooks`, and any timeline-drop hook directory you modify.

## Deliverables

- All changed files with the wiring in place.
- New/updated tests covering both branches.
- A short note in your final summary listing every caller of `processDroppedImages` and confirming each one still works.

# Implementation Plan: Wire Timeline Drop to the Local-File-Handle Flow

## Overview
The timeline drop path (`useUnifiedDrop` → `useDropActions.handleTimelineImageDrop` → `useHandleExternalImageDrop` mutation → `processDroppedImages` → `processSingleDroppedImage` → `createGenerationForUploadedImage`) unconditionally uploads to Supabase Storage and never captures `FileSystemFileHandle`s. The gallery drop path (`useDropToGeneration`) already implements the local-handle flow against the same `IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES` gate, calling `createGenerationForLocalFile` for large images.

We will: (1) capture handles synchronously at the timeline drop event, (2) thread an optional `Array<FileSystemFileHandle | null>` parallel to `imageFiles` through the timeline pipeline into `processDroppedImages`/`processSingleDroppedImage`, and (3) reuse the `useDropToGeneration` gate (`size ≥ IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES` AND a usable handle) to branch into `createGenerationForLocalFile`. All non-timeline callers of `processDroppedImages` (`ShotListView`, `App.tsx`, `useModalImageHandlers`, shot-creation flows) keep working because the new param is optional and defaults to absent → upload path.

The crop steps complicate things: `useDropActions.handleTimelineImageDrop` calls `cropImagesToShotAspectRatio` *before* invoking the mutation, and `processDroppedImages` then calls `cropFilesIfNeeded` again. Both can return *new* `File`s. We use reference equality (`processedFile === originalFile`) at each crop boundary and null out the handle if the bytes diverged.

## Constraints
- New param everywhere is optional. No behavior change for callers that don't supply it.
- Keep the gate logic identical to `useDropToGeneration`: `file.size >= IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES` AND `getAsFileSystemHandle()` resolves to a kind-`file` handle with `getFile`.
- Don't refactor unrelated code; don't add comments justifying the change.
- Pre-existing tsc error in `useDropToGeneration.ts` (FileSystemFileHandle missing `queryPermission`/`requestPermission` lib types) is acceptable to mirror.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Capture handles at the drop boundary (`src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/hooks/drag/useUnifiedDrop.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Extend** `UseUnifiedDropProps.onFileDrop` to accept an optional 3rd argument: `handles?: Array<FileSystemFileHandle | null>` (`useUnifiedDrop.ts:18`).
2. **In `handleDrop`**, in the file-drop branch, synchronously snapshot `e.dataTransfer.items` filtered by `kind === 'file'` BEFORE any await, and immediately invoke `getAsFileSystemHandle()` on each (storing the returned promise). This matches the `useDropToGeneration` pattern (`useDropToGeneration.ts:60-77`) but kicks off the promises during the synchronous portion of the event handler:
   ```ts
   const allFiles = Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files);
   const fileItems = Array.from(e.dataTransfer.items ?? []).filter(it => it.kind === 'file');
   const handlePromises = fileItems.map(it => (it as DataTransferItem & {
     getAsFileSystemHandle?: () => Promise<FileSystemHandle | null>;
   }).getAsFileSystemHandle?.() ?? Promise.resolve(null));
   ```
3. **Filter** the parallel arrays together with `filterValidTimelineImageFiles`. Replace the existing `validFiles = filterValidTimelineImageFiles(files)` with a paired filter that retains handle promises only at indices whose file passed validation.
4. **Resolve** the handle promises (`await Promise.all`), guard with the same `isReadableFileHandle` check from the gallery hook (kind === 'file' && getFile present), and pass the resolved `Array<FileSystemFileHandle | null>` as the third arg to `onFileDrop`.
5. **Add** a small private helper `supportsLocalFileHandles()` mirroring `useDropToGeneration.ts:41-44`. If `false`, skip the `getAsFileSystemHandle` calls and pass `undefined`.

### Step 2: Update `FileDropHandler` and propagate the optional handles arg through plumbing (`src/shared/types/imageHandlers.ts`, `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/TimelineContainer/components/{TimelineBottomControls,TimelineTrackPrelude,TimelineTrackContent,TimelineControls}.tsx`, `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/TimelineContainer/TimelineContainer.tsx`, `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/hooks/segment/useTrailingEndpoint.ts`, `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/hooks/drag/useEmptyStateDrop.ts`, `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/sections/TimelineSection.tsx`, `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/VideoGenerationModalSections.tsx`, `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/editor-state/useShotSettingsValue.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Change** `FileDropHandler` to `(files: File[], targetFrame?: number, handles?: Array<FileSystemFileHandle | null>) => Promise<void>` (`src/shared/types/imageHandlers.ts:15`).
2. **Mirror** the same expanded signature on every locally-typed `onFileDrop?: (files, targetFrame?) => Promise<void>` declaration: `useEmptyStateDrop.ts:13`, `useUnifiedDrop.ts:18`, `useTrailingEndpoint.ts:17`, `TimelineBottomControls.tsx:14`, `TimelineTrackPrelude.tsx:52`. Existing callers that pass only `(files)` or `(files, frame)` keep compiling.
3. **At intermediate forwarding points** that already invoke `onFileDrop(files, frame)` (e.g. `useTrailingEndpoint.ts:141`, `TimelineBottomControls.tsx:106` Plus button), leave call sites unchanged — the handles arg is optional so synthetic File sources (canvas blob, file input) correctly fall through to upload path.
4. **In `useShotSettingsValue.ts:210-212`**, update the `onFileDrop` wrapper to accept and forward the third arg:
   ```ts
   onFileDrop: async (files, targetFrame, handles) => {
     await generationActions.handleTimelineImageDrop(files, targetFrame, handles);
   }
   ```

### Step 3: Wire through `useDropActions.handleTimelineImageDrop` and re-align handles after the local crop step (`src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/actions/useDropActions.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Extend** `handleTimelineImageDrop`'s signature to `(files: File[], targetFrame?: number, handles?: Array<FileSystemFileHandle | null>)` (`useDropActions.ts:187`).
2. **Re-align** handles to processed files after `cropImagesToShotAspectRatio` (`useDropActions.ts:252-258`):
   ```ts
   const processedFiles = await cropImagesToShotAspectRatio(files, ...);
   const alignedHandles = handles?.map((h, i) =>
     processedFiles[i] === files[i] ? h : null
   );
   ```
3. **Pass** `alignedHandles` into the mutation payload (`useDropActions.ts:266-273`) as a new `handles?` field on `ExternalImageDropVariables`.
4. **Mirror** the same signature on `useGenerationActions.ts:61-79` (the wrapper that returns `handleTimelineImageDrop`) so the type flows up to `useShotSettingsValue`'s `GenerationActionsReturn` interface (`useShotSettingsValue.ts:33`). Update that interface as well.

### Step 4: Add the local-handle branch to `processDroppedImages` / `processSingleDroppedImage` (`src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Extend** `ExternalImageDropVariables` with `handles?: Array<FileSystemFileHandle | null>` (`externalImageDrop.ts:9-17`). Optional → batch/app/modal/shot-list callers need no change.
2. **Re-align** handles to processed files after `cropFilesIfNeeded`:
   ```ts
   const processedFiles = await cropFilesIfNeeded(imageFiles, cropConfig);
   const alignedHandles = variables.handles?.map((h, i) =>
     processedFiles[i] === imageFiles[i] ? h : null
   );
   ```
3. **Pass** the per-index handle into `processSingleDroppedImage` (`externalImageDrop.ts:336-346`) as a new optional `handle` arg.
4. **Branch** in `processSingleDroppedImage` (`externalImageDrop.ts:248-310`): if `imageFile.size >= IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES` AND `handle != null`, call `createGenerationForLocalFile({ file: imageFile, projectId, handle, mediaType: 'image' })` to produce the generation row. Otherwise keep the existing `createGeneration(...)` upload path. Pass the resulting `generation` into `attachGenerationToShot` unchanged. Note that the local path produces `location: null` and a real `thumbnail_url` — `attachGenerationToShot` already tolerates `generation.location || undefined`.
5. **Import** `createGenerationForLocalFile` and `IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES` in `externalImageDrop.ts`.

### Step 5: Update / add tests (`src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.test.ts`, `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/actions/__tests__/useDropActions.test.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **In `externalImageDrop.test.ts`** add two cases mirroring the existing setup:
   - **Large file + handle → local-handle path:** mock `createGenerationForLocalFile` (mocked module `@/shared/lib/media/createGenerationFromFile`), pass a 26 MB file (`Object.defineProperty(file, 'size', { value: 26*1024*1024 })`) and a stub `FileSystemFileHandle`-shaped object with `kind: 'file'`. Assert `createGenerationForLocalFile` was called once and `uploadImageWithThumbnail` / `createGenerationForUploadedImage` was NOT.
   - **Small file or no handle → upload path:** assert the existing upload mocks still fire and `createGenerationForLocalFile` was NOT called. Cover both sub-cases (small-file-with-handle, large-file-without-handle).
2. **In `useDropActions.test.ts`** add a case verifying that `handleTimelineImageDrop(files, frame, handles)` forwards the `handles` array into `handleExternalImageDropMutateAsyncMock` and that, when `cropImagesToShotAspectRatio` returns a *different* `File` reference, the corresponding handle is nulled out before forwarding.

### Step 6: Verify (terminal)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the targeted suites first:
   - `npx vitest run src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.test.ts`
   - `npx vitest run src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/actions/__tests__/useDropActions.test.ts`
   - `npx vitest run src/features/gallery/hooks/useDropToGeneration.test.tsx`
2. **Run** broader vitest on the touched directories:
   - `npx vitest run src/shared/hooks/shots src/features/gallery/hooks src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline`
3. **Run** `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json` and confirm no NEW errors beyond the pre-existing `FileSystemFileHandle.queryPermission/requestPermission` pattern.

## Execution Order
1. Add the optional `handles` plumbing types and the synchronous capture in `useUnifiedDrop` first — these are invisible until consumed.
2. Wire through `useDropActions` → `ExternalImageDropVariables` → `processSingleDroppedImage` next.
3. Add tests last so they exercise the full branch.

## Validation Order
1. `externalImageDrop.test.ts` (cheapest unit-level check of the branch).
2. `useDropActions.test.ts` (verifies plumbing + crop re-alignment).
3. `useDropToGeneration.test.tsx` (regression — gallery path must still pass).
4. Broader directory vitest sweep.
5. `tsc --noEmit` last.

## Caller Audit (deliverable note)
`processDroppedImages` is invoked only via `useHandleExternalImageDrop` (`src/shared/hooks/shots/useShotCreation.ts:78`). Mutation callers:
- `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/actions/useDropActions.ts:142` — **timeline path; new behavior**.
- `src/app/App.tsx:38` — app-level external drop into a shot. No `handles` supplied → upload path (unchanged).
- `src/tools/travel-between-images/pages/ShotListView.tsx:70` — shot-list batch drop. No `handles` → upload path (unchanged).
- `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/hooks/useModalImageHandlers.ts:33` — modal flow. No `handles` → upload path (unchanged).
- Tests in `src/shared/hooks/__tests__/useShotCreation.test.ts`, `src/shared/hooks/shots/__tests__/useShotCreation.test.ts`, `src/tools/travel-between-images/pages/ShotListView.test.tsx` mock the mutation directly; no payload-shape change needed for them.

      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the no-duration-fetch decision against `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/config-utils.ts:17-35`, and `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/visualclip.tsx:114-123`. the repository still derives clip length from `to - from`, and the final-video query still provides no duration metadata, so the revised plan still cannot prove that `to: endtime - starttime` is safe for shorter source media; a short final video can still be asked to play longer than its actual content.",

      {
        "id": "DEBT-041",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual export surface in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/llm/messages.ts` and its consumers. step 6 says to 'remove local `isrecord`' from `messages.ts` and import it from `utils.ts`, but without also preserving `messages.ts` as an exporter or rewiring `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, that change would break the module graph immediately.",

      {
        "id": "DEBT-055",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-audio-reactive-effects-to-20260406-0440"
        ]
      },

        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-audio-reactive-effects-to-20260406-0440"

      },

      {
        "id": "DEBT-070",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked revised step 2.4 against `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useclipresize.ts:76-100`. the plan still says to clamp audio by writing back to `action.end`, but the current hook treats the callback `action` as input data and then creates fresh row actions from it. mutating that caller-owned object is unnecessary and risks coupling the resize hook to outside references instead of using a local `clampedend` variable.",

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the no-duration-fetch decision against `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/config-utils.ts:17-35`, and `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/visualclip.tsx:114-123`. the repository still derives clip length from `to - from`, and the final-video query still provides no duration metadata, so the revised plan still cannot prove that `to: endtime - starttime` is safe for shorter source media; a short final video can still be asked to play longer than its actual content.",

      {

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` design is still underspecified against `materializedata()`. `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so if the executor literally follows step 2.4 and overwrites `pinnedshotgroups` on an already-materialized `timelinedata` before `commitdata()`, the saved config and the cached `stablesignature` will diverge unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config.",

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the grouped-drag plan against `useclipdrag` and `usetimelinetrackmanagement.movecliptorow()`. the plan fixes the pointer-down selection regression, but it still does not say how `pinnedshotgroup.trackid` is updated when a grouped drag or move lands on a different row, so the overlay and menu state can still point at the old track after a successful cross-track move.",

16	
17	function isImageFile(file: File): boolean {
18	  return file.type.startsWith('image/');
19	}
20	
21	function isVideoFile(file: File): boolean {
22	  return file.type.startsWith('video/');
23	}
24	
25	function formatLimitMb(bytes: number): number {
26	  return Math.round(bytes / (1024 * 1024));
27	}
28	
29	type FileDropHandleItem = DataTransferItem & {
30	  getAsFileSystemHandle?: () => Promise<FileSystemHandle | null>;
31	};
32	
33	type FileSystemHandleLike = FileSystemHandle & {
34	  getFile?: () => Promise<File>;
35	};
36	
37	interface DropToGenerationOptions {
38	  items?: Iterable<DataTransferItem>;
39	}
40	
41	function supportsLocalFileHandles(): boolean {
42	  return typeof DataTransferItem !== 'undefined'
43	    && typeof (DataTransferItem.prototype as FileDropHandleItem).getAsFileSystemHandle === 'function';
44	}
45	
46	function isReadableFileHandle(handle: FileSystemHandleLike | null): handle is FileSystemFileHandle {
47	  return !!handle && handle.kind === 'file' && typeof handle.getFile === 'function';
48	}
49	
50	export function useDropToGeneration(): (files: File[], options?: DropToGenerationOptions) => Promise<void> {
51	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
52	  const { selectedProjectId } = useProjectSelectionContext();
53	
54	  return useCallback(async (files: File[], options?: DropToGenerationOptions) => {
55	    if (!selectedProjectId) {
56	      toast.error('Please select a project first');
57	      return;
58	    }
59	
60	    const dropItems = Array.from(options?.items ?? []).filter((item): item is FileDropHandleItem => item.kind === 'file');
61	    const canUseLocalHandles = supportsLocalFileHandles();
62	    let insertedCount = 0;
63	
64	    for (const [index, file] of files.entries()) {
65	      try {
66	        if (isImageFile(file)) {
67	          if (file.size >= IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES) {
68	            const item = dropItems[index];
69	            if (!canUseLocalHandles || !item?.getAsFileSystemHandle) {
70	              toast.info(
71	                `${file.name} is ${formatLimitMb(IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES)}MB+ and will use the browser fallback flow in a later batch.`,
72	              );
73	              continue;
74	            }
75	
76	            const handle = await item.getAsFileSystemHandle();
77	            if (!isReadableFileHandle(handle)) {
78	              toast.info(
79	                `${file.name} could not keep a local file handle. The browser fallback flow will arrive in a later batch.`,
80	              );
81	              continue;
82	            }
83	
84	            await createGenerationForLocalFile({
85	              file,
86	              projectId: selectedProjectId,
87	              handle,
88	              mediaType: 'image',
89	            });
90	            insertedCount += 1;
91	            continue;
92	          }
93	
94	          await createGenerationForUploadedImage({
95	            imageFile: file,
96	            projectId: selectedProjectId,
97	          });
98	          insertedCount += 1;
99	          continue;
100	        }
101	
102	        if (isVideoFile(file)) {
103	          if (file.size >= VIDEO_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES) {
104	            const item = dropItems[index];
105	            if (!canUseLocalHandles || !item?.getAsFileSystemHandle) {
106	              toast.info(
107	                `${file.name} is ${formatLimitMb(VIDEO_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES)}MB+ and will use the browser fallback flow in a later batch.`,
108	              );
109	              continue;
110	            }
111	
112	            const handle = await item.getAsFileSystemHandle();
113	            if (!isReadableFileHandle(handle)) {
114	              toast.info(
115	                `${file.name} could not keep a local file handle. The browser fallback flow will arrive in a later batch.`,
116	              );
117	              continue;
118	            }
119	
120	            await createGenerationForLocalFile({
121	              file,
122	              projectId: selectedProjectId,
123	              handle,
124	              mediaType: 'video',
125	            });
126	            insertedCount += 1;
127	            continue;
128	          }
129	
130	          await createGenerationForUploadedVideo({
131	            videoFile: file,
132	            projectId: selectedProjectId,
133	          });
134	          insertedCount += 1;
135	          continue;
136	        }
137	
138	        toast.error(`Unsupported file type: ${file.name}`);
139	      } catch (error) {
140	        normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
141	          context: 'useDropToGeneration',
142	          toastTitle: `Failed to add ${file.name}`,
143	        });
144	      }
145	    }
146	
147	    if (insertedCount > 0) {
148	      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({
149	        [REDACTED](selectedProjectId),
150	      });
151	    }
152	  }, [queryClient, selectedProjectId]);
153	}

1	import React from 'react';

5	import { UI_Z_LAYERS } from '@/shared/lib/uiLayers';
6	import { usePanesStore } from '@/shared/state/panesStore';
7	import { setDragging, useIsDraggingFiles } from '@/shared/state/dragOverlayStore';
8	import { useGenerationsPaneController } from '../hooks/useGenerationsPaneController';
9	import { useDropToGeneration } from '@/features/gallery/hooks/useDropToGeneration';
10	
11	type GenerationsPaneController = ReturnType<typeof useGenerationsPaneController>;
12	
13	const CHIP_VERTICAL_OFFSET_PX = 56;
14	
15	export function GenerationsDropChip({
16	  controller,
17	}: {
18	  controller: GenerationsPaneController;
19	}): React.ReactElement | null {
20	  const { pane } = controller;
21	  const isDraggingFiles = useIsDraggingFiles();
22	  const dropToGeneration = useDropToGeneration();
23	  const effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight = usePanesStore((state) => state.effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight);
24	  const isVisible = pane.isLocked || (pane.paneIsOpen && !pane.isLocked);
25	
26	  const dynamicStyle = usePositionStrategy({
27	    side: 'bottom',
28	    dimension: effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight,
29	    offsets: {
30	      horizontal:
31	        (pane.isShotsPaneLocked ? pane.shotsPaneWidth : 0) -
32	        (pane.isTasksPaneLocked ? pane.tasksPaneWidth : 0),
33	    },
34	    isVisible,
35	  });
36	
37	  if (!isDraggingFiles || pane.isOnImageGenerationPage) {
38	    return null;
39	  }
40	
41	  const transform = typeof dynamicStyle.transform === 'string'
42	    ? `${dynamicStyle.transform} translateY(-${CHIP_VERTICAL_OFFSET_PX}px)`
43	    : `translateX(-50%) translateY(-${CHIP_VERTICAL_OFFSET_PX}px)`;
44	
45	  return (
46	    <div
47	      data-testid="generations-drop-chip"
48	      aria-hidden="true"
49	      onDragOver={(event) => {
50	        event.preventDefault();
51	      }}
52	      onDrop={(event) => {
53	        event.preventDefault();
54	        const files = Array.from(event.dataTransfer?.files ?? []);
55	        const items = Array.from(event.dataTransfer?.items ?? []);
56	        setDragging(false);
57	        if (files.length === 0) {
58	          return;
59	        }
60	        void dropToGeneration(files, { items });
61	      }}
62	      style={{
63	        ...dynamicStyle,
64	        transform,
65	        zIndex: UI_Z_LAYERS.GENERATIONS_DROP_CHIP,
66	      }}
67	      className={cn(
68	        'fixed flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900/95 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-zinc-100 shadow-xl backdrop-blur-sm',
69	        'pointer-events-auto touch-none',
70	      )}
71	    >
72	      <Upload className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-300" />
73	      <span>Drop to add as generation</span>
74	    </div>
75	  );
76	}

21	  fullRange: number;
22	}
23	
24	/**
25	 * Unified drop hook that handles both file drops (from file system) and generation drops (from GenerationsPane)
26	 * Reuses the same coordinate system and visual feedback for consistency
27	 */
28	export const useUnifiedDrop = ({
29	  onFileDrop,
30	  onGenerationDrop,
31	  fullMin,
32	  fullRange
33	}: UseUnifiedDropProps) => {
34	  const [isFileOver, setIsFileOver] = useState(false);
35	  const [isGenerationOver, setIsGenerationOver] = useState(false);
36	  const [dropTargetFrame, setDropTargetFrame] = useState<number | null>(null);
37	
38	  /**
39	   * Detect the type of item being dragged (wrapper around shared utility for logging)
40	   */
41	  const getDragType = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>): DragType => {
42	    const dragType = sharedGetDragType(e);
43	    
44	    return dragType;
45	  }, []);
46	
47	  const handleDragEnter = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
48	    e.preventDefault();
49	    e.stopPropagation();
50	    
51	    const dragType = getDragType(e);
52	    
53	    if (dragType === 'file' && onFileDrop) {
54	      setIsFileOver(true);
55	    } else if (dragType === 'generation' && onGenerationDrop) {
56	      setIsGenerationOver(true);
57	    }
58	  }, [getDragType, onFileDrop, onGenerationDrop]);
59	
60	  const handleDragOver = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>, containerRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>) => {
61	    e.preventDefault();
62	    e.stopPropagation();
63	    
64	    const dragType = getDragType(e);
65	    
66	    if (dragType !== 'none' && containerRef.current) {
67	      const rect = containerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
68	      // Account for timeline padding offset - same calculation as useTimelineDrag
69	      const relativeX = e.clientX - rect.left - TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET;
70	      const effectiveWidth = rect.width - (TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET * 2);
71	      const targetFrame = Math.max(0, pixelToFrame(relativeX, effectiveWidth, fullMin, fullRange));
72	      setDropTargetFrame(targetFrame);
73	      
74	      if (dragType === 'file' && onFileDrop) {
75	        setIsFileOver(true);
76	        e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy';
77	      } else if (dragType === 'generation' && onGenerationDrop) {
78	        setIsGenerationOver(true);
79	        e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy';
80	      } else {
81	        e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'none';
82	      }
83	    } else {
84	      e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'none';
85	      setDropTargetFrame(null);
86	    }
87	  }, [getDragType, onFileDrop, onGenerationDrop, fullMin, fullRange]);
88	
89	  const handleDragLeave = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
90	    e.preventDefault();
91	    e.stopPropagation();
92	    
93	    // Only clear state if we're actually leaving the container
94	    if (e.currentTarget.contains(e.relatedTarget as Node)) {
95	      return;
96	    }
97	    
98	    setIsFileOver(false);
99	    setIsGenerationOver(false);
100	    setDropTargetFrame(null);
101	  }, []);
102	
103	  const handleDrop = useCallback(async (e: React.DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>, containerRef?: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>) => {
104	    e.preventDefault();
105	    e.stopPropagation();
106	    
107	    const dragType = getDragType(e);
108	    
109	    // Calculate target frame directly from drop coordinates (not stale state)
110	    // This fixes the "jumping to wrong location" bug caused by stale dropTargetFrame state
111	    let targetFrame: number | null = dropTargetFrame;
112	    if (containerRef?.current) {
113	      const rect = containerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
114	      const relativeX = e.clientX - rect.left - TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET;
115	      const effectiveWidth = rect.width - (TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET * 2);
116	      targetFrame = Math.max(0, pixelToFrame(relativeX, effectiveWidth, fullMin, fullRange));
117	    }
118	    
119	    // Reset visual state — always, even if a child variant handler already
120	    // processed the drop. This is the whole point: the event bubbles up so we
121	    // can clean up, but we skip re-processing the action.
122	    setIsFileOver(false);
123	    setIsGenerationOver(false);
124	    setDropTargetFrame(null);
125	
126	    // A child variant drop target already handled this drop — don't also
127	    // create a standalone image.
128	    if (wasDropHandledByVariant(e)) {
129	      return;
130	    }
131	
132	    // Handle file drops (from file system)
133	    if (dragType === 'file' && onFileDrop) {
134	      const files = Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files);
135	      
136	      if (files.length === 0) {
137	        return;
138	      }
139	
140	      const validFiles = filterValidTimelineImageFiles(files);
141	
142	      if (validFiles.length === 0) {
143	        return;
144	      }
145	
146	      try {
147	        await onFileDrop(validFiles, targetFrame ?? undefined);
148	      } catch (error) {
149	        normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'UnifiedDrop', toastTitle: 'Failed to add images' });
150	      }
151	    }
152	    
153	    // Handle generation drops (from GenerationsPane)
154	    else if (dragType === 'generation' && onGenerationDrop) {
155	      
156	      const data = getGenerationDropData(e);
157	      
158	      if (!data) {
159	        normalizeAndPresentError(new Error('No valid data found'), { context: 'UnifiedDrop', showToast: false });
160	        return;
161	      }
162	      
163	      try {
164	        
165	        await onGenerationDrop(data.generationId, data.imageUrl, data.thumbUrl, targetFrame ?? undefined);
166	      } catch (error) {
167	        normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'UnifiedDrop', toastTitle: 'Failed to add generation' });
168	      }
169	    }
170	  }, [getDragType, onFileDrop, onGenerationDrop, dropTargetFrame, fullMin, fullRange]);
171	
172	  // Determine current drag type for consumers
173	  const currentDragType: DragType = isFileOver ? 'file' : isGenerationOver ? 'generation' : 'none';
174	
175	  return {
176	    isFileOver: isFileOver || isGenerationOver, // Combined state for backward compatibility
177	    dropTargetFrame,
178	    dragType: currentDragType,
179	    handleDragEnter,
180	    handleDragOver,
181	    handleDragLeave,
182	    handleDrop,
183	  };
184	};
185

76	async function getCropConfig(projectId: string, shotId: string | null): Promise<CropConfig> {
77	  const config: CropConfig = {
78	    shouldCrop: true,
79	    targetAspectRatio: null,
80	    aspectRatioSource: 'none',
81	  };
82	
83	  try {
84	    const { data: projectData } = await supabase().from('projects')
85	      .select('aspect_ratio, settings')
86	      .eq('id', projectId)
87	      .single();
88	
89	    let shotAspectRatio: string | null = null;
90	    if (shotId) {
91	      const { data: shotData } = await supabase().from('shots')
92	        .select('aspect_ratio')
93	        .eq('id', shotId)
94	        .single();
95	      shotAspectRatio = shotData?.aspect_ratio || null;
96	    }
97	
98	    const uploadSettings = (projectData?.settings as Record<string, unknown> | null)?.upload as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
99	    const cropToProjectSize = uploadSettings?.cropToProjectSize;
100	    config.shouldCrop = typeof cropToProjectSize === 'boolean' ? cropToProjectSize : true;
101	
102	    const effectiveRatioStr = shotAspectRatio || projectData?.aspect_ratio || null;
103	    if (effectiveRatioStr) {
104	      config.targetAspectRatio = parseRatio(effectiveRatioStr);
105	      config.aspectRatioSource = shotAspectRatio ? 'shot' : 'project';
106	    }
107	  } catch (error) {
108	    normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useShotCreation:aspectRatio', showToast: false });
109	  }
110	
111	  return config;
112	}

114	async function cropFilesIfNeeded(imageFiles: File[], config: CropConfig): Promise<File[]> {
115	  const { shouldCrop, targetAspectRatio } = config;
116	  if (!shouldCrop || !targetAspectRatio || Number.isNaN(targetAspectRatio)) {
117	    return imageFiles;
118	  }
119	
120	  try {
121	    const cropPromises = imageFiles.map(async (file) => {
122	      try {
123	        if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
124	          return file;
125	        }
126	
127	        const result = await cropImageToProjectAspectRatio(file, targetAspectRatio);
128	        return result?.croppedFile || file;
129	      } catch (error) {
130	        normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: `useShotCreation:crop:${file.name}`, showToast: false });
131	        return file;
132	      }
133	    });
134	
135	    return await Promise.all(cropPromises);
136	  } catch (error) {
137	    normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useShotCreation:batchCrop', showToast: false });
138	    return imageFiles;
139	  }
140	}

143	  targetShotId: string | null,
144	  currentShotCount: number,

147	): Promise<string | null> {

167	async function attachGenerationToShot(input: {
168	  shotId: string;
169	  generation: Database['public']['Tables']['generations']['Row'];
170	  projectId: string;
171	  thumbnailUrl?: string | null;
172	  fileIndex: number;
173	  variables: ExternalImageDropVariables;
174	  addImageToShot: ProcessDroppedImagesInput['addImageToShot'];
175	  addImageToShotWithoutPosition: ProcessDroppedImagesInput['addImageToShotWithoutPosition'];
176	}): Promise<{ shot_generation_id: string; timeline_frame: number | null }> {
177	  const {
178	    shotId,
179	    generation,
180	    projectId,
181	    thumbnailUrl,
182	    fileIndex,
183	    variables,
184	    addImageToShot,
185	    addImageToShotWithoutPosition,
186	  } = input;
187	
188	  const { positions, skipAutoPosition } = variables;
189	  const explicitPosition = positions && positions.length > fileIndex ? positions[fileIndex] : undefined;
190	  const baseInput = {
191	    shot_id: shotId,
192	    generation_id: generation.id as string,
193	    project_id: projectId,
194	    imageUrl: generation.location || undefined,
195	    thumbUrl: thumbnailUrl || generation.location || undefined,
196	  };
197	
198	  if (explicitPosition !== undefined) {
199	    const result = await addImageToShot({
200	      ...baseInput,
201	      timelineFrame: explicitPosition,
202	    });
203	    const shotGenerationId = typeof (result as { id?: unknown })?.id === 'string'
204	      ? (result as { id: string }).id
205	      : null;
206	    if (!shotGenerationId) {
207	      throw new Error('Failed to attach uploaded generation with explicit position.');
208	    }
209	    return {
210	      shot_generation_id: shotGenerationId,
211	      timeline_frame: typeof (result as { timeline_frame?: unknown })?.timeline_frame === 'number'
212	        ? (result as { timeline_frame: number }).timeline_frame
213	        : explicitPosition,
214	    };
215	  }
216	
217	  if (skipAutoPosition) {
218	    const result = await addImageToShotWithoutPosition(baseInput);
219	    const shotGenerationId = typeof (result as { id?: unknown })?.id === 'string'
220	      ? (result as { id: string }).id
221	      : null;
222	    if (!shotGenerationId) {
223	      throw new Error('Failed to attach uploaded generation without position.');
224	    }
225	    return {
226	      shot_generation_id: shotGenerationId,
227	      timeline_frame: null,
228	    };
229	  }
230	
231	  const result = await addImageToShot({
232	    ...baseInput,
233	  });
234	  const shotGenerationId = typeof (result as { id?: unknown })?.id === 'string'
235	    ? (result as { id: string }).id
236	    : null;
237	  if (!shotGenerationId) {
238	    throw new Error('Failed to attach uploaded generation to shot.');
239	  }
240	  return {
241	    shot_generation_id: shotGenerationId,
242	    timeline_frame: typeof (result as { timeline_frame?: unknown })?.timeline_frame === 'number'
243	      ? (result as { timeline_frame: number }).timeline_frame
244	      : null,
245	  };
246	}

258	}): Promise<UploadedGenerationMetadata | null> {

260	    imageFile,
261	    fileIndex,
262	    totalFiles,
263	    shotId,
264	    projectId,
265	    variables,
266	    addImageToShot,
267	    addImageToShotWithoutPosition,

271	  let generation: Database['public']['Tables']['generations']['Row'];
272	  try {
273	    generation = await createGeneration({

276	      onProgress: buildProgressHandler(variables.onProgress, fileIndex, totalFiles),
277	    });

279	    toast.error(`Failed to create generation data for ${imageFile.name}: ${(error as Error).message}`);
280	    return null;
281	  }
282	
283	  if (!generation?.id) {
284	    toast.error(`Failed to create generation record for ${imageFile.name}.`);
285	    return null;
286	  }
287	
288	  const attachment = await attachGenerationToShot({
289	    shotId,
290	    generation,
291	    projectId,

293	    fileIndex,
294	    variables,
295	    addImageToShot,
296	    addImageToShotWithoutPosition,
297	  });
298	
299	  return {
300	    generationId: generation.id,
301	    location: generation.location,
302	    thumbnail_url: generation.thumbnail_url ?? generation.location,
303	    type: generation.type,
304	    created_at: generation.created_at,
305	    params: generation.params,
306	    primary_variant_id: generation.primary_variant_id,
307	    shot_generation_id: attachment.shot_generation_id,
308	    timeline_frame: attachment.timeline_frame,
309	  };
310	}
311	export async function processDroppedImages(
312	  input: ProcessDroppedImagesInput,
313	): Promise<{ shotId: string; generationIds: string[]; generationMetadata: UploadedGenerationMetadata[] } | null> {
314	  const {
315	    variables,
316	    projectId,
317	    createShot,
318	    addImageToShot,
319	    addImageToShotWithoutPosition,
320	    createGeneration = createGenerationForUploadedImage,
321	  } = input;
322	  const { imageFiles, targetShotId, currentShotCount } = variables;
323	
324	  const cropConfig = await getCropConfig(projectId, targetShotId);
325	  const processedFiles = await cropFilesIfNeeded(imageFiles, cropConfig);
326	  const shotId = await ensureTargetShotId(targetShotId, currentShotCount, projectId, createShot);
327	  if (!shotId) {
328	    return null;
329	  }
330	
331	  const generationIds: string[] = [];
332	  const generationMetadata: UploadedGenerationMetadata[] = [];
333	  for (let fileIndex = 0; fileIndex < processedFiles.length; fileIndex++) {
334	    const imageFile = processedFiles[fileIndex];
335	    try {
336	      const metadata = await processSingleDroppedImage({
337	        imageFile,
338	        fileIndex,
339	        totalFiles: processedFiles.length,
340	        shotId,
341	        projectId,
342	        variables,
343	        addImageToShot,
344	        addImageToShotWithoutPosition,
345	        createGeneration,
346	      });
347	      if (metadata) {
348	        generationIds.push(metadata.generationId);
349	        generationMetadata.push(metadata);
350	      }
351	    } catch (error) {
352	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useShotCreation', toastTitle: `Failed to process file ${imageFile.name}` });
353	    }
354	  }
355	
356	  return generationIds.length > 0 ? { shotId, generationIds, generationMetadata } : null;
357	}

51	async function readImageDimensions(file: File): Promise<{ width: number; height: number } | null> {
52	  try {
53	    return await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
54	      const reader = new FileReader();
55	
56	      reader.onload = (event) => {
57	        const img = new Image();
58	
59	        img.onload = () => {
60	          resolve({ width: img.width, height: img.height });
61	        };
62	
63	        img.onerror = () => {
64	          reject(new Error('Failed to load dropped image.'));
65	        };
66	
67	        if (event.target?.result) {
68	          img.src = event.target.result as string;
69	          return;
70	        }
71	
72	        reject(new Error('Failed to read dropped image.'));
73	      };
74	
75	      reader.onerror = () => {
76	        reject(new Error('Failed to probe dropped image dimensions.'));
77	      };
78	
79	      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
80	    });
81	  } catch (error) {
82	    normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
83	      context: 'DropAutoAdjustShotAspectRatio:probe',
84	      showToast: false,
85	    });
86	    return null;
87	  }
88	}
89	
90	async function maybeAutoAdjustShotAspectRatio(
91	  files: File[],
92	  currentShot: Shot,
93	  projectsList: ProjectWithSettings[],
94	  projectId: string,
95	): Promise<AutoAdjustedAspectRatio | null> {
96	  try {
97	    const firstFile = files[0];
98	    if (!firstFile) {
99	      return null;
100	    }
101	
102	    const dimensions = await readImageDimensions(firstFile);
103	    if (!dimensions?.width || !dimensions.height) {
104	      return null;
105	    }
106	
107	    const imageRatio = dimensions.width / dimensions.height;
108	    const closestAspectRatio = findClosestAspectRatio(imageRatio);
109	    const currentProject = projectsList.find((project) => project.id === projectId);
110	    const currentAspectRatio =
111	      currentShot.aspect_ratio ??
112	      currentProject?.aspectRatio ??
113	      currentProject?.settings?.aspectRatio ??
114	      '16:9';
115	
116	    if (closestAspectRatio === currentAspectRatio) {
117	      return null;
118	    }
119	
120	    return {
121	      previousAspectRatio: currentShot.aspect_ratio ?? null,
122	      adjustedTo: closestAspectRatio,
123	    };
124	  } catch (error) {
125	    normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
126	      context: 'DropAutoAdjustShotAspectRatio',
127	      showToast: false,
128	    });
129	    return null;
130	  }
131	}

133	export const useDropActions = ({
134	  actions,

136	  projectId,
137	  batchVideoFrames,
138	}: UseDropActionsProps) => {

146	
147	  // Stability refs - prevent callback recreation when data/mutation state changes

184	  /**
185	   * Handle dropping external image files onto the timeline
186	   */
187	  const handleTimelineImageDrop = useCallback(async (files: File[], targetFrame?: number) => {
188	    const currentShot = selectedShotRef.current;
189	    const currentProjectId = projectIdRef.current;
190	    const currentBatchVideoFrames = batchVideoFramesRef.current;
191	    let adjustedShot: Shot | undefined;
192	
193	    if (!currentShot?.id || !currentProjectId) {
194	      toast.error("Cannot add images: No shot or project selected.");
195	      return;
196	    }
197	
198	    try {
199	      try {
200	        const autoAdjustedAspectRatio = await maybeAutoAdjustShotAspectRatio(
201	          files,
202	          currentShot,
203	          projectsRef.current as ProjectWithSettings[],
204	          currentProjectId
205	        );
206	
207	        if (autoAdjustedAspectRatio) {
208	          const ok = await updateShotAspectRatioRef.current(
209	            currentShot.id,
210	            currentProjectId,
211	            autoAdjustedAspectRatio.adjustedTo,
212	            { immediate: true }
213	          );
214	
215	          if (ok) {
216	            setAutoAdjustedAspectRatioRef.current(autoAdjustedAspectRatio);
217	            adjustedShot = {
218	              ...currentShot,
219	              aspect_ratio: autoAdjustedAspectRatio.adjustedTo,
220	            };
221	          } else {
222	            normalizeAndPresentError(
223	              new Error('Failed to auto-adjust shot aspect ratio for the dropped image.'),
224	              {
225	                context: 'DropAutoAdjustShotAspectRatio:update',
226	                showToast: false,
227	                logData: {
228	                  shotId: currentShot.id,
229	                  adjustedTo: autoAdjustedAspectRatio.adjustedTo,
230	                },
231	              }
232	            );
233	          }
234	        }
235	      } catch (error) {
236	        normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
237	          context: 'DropAutoAdjustShotAspectRatio:update',
238	          showToast: false,
239	          logData: { shotId: currentShot.id },
240	        });
241	      }
242	
243	      actionsRef.current.setUploadingImage(true);
244	
245	      // 1. Calculate target positions BEFORE upload
246	      const calculatedTargetFrame = await fetchNextAvailableFrameForShot(
247	        currentShot.id,
248	        targetFrame
249	      );
250	
251	      // 2. Crop images to shot aspect ratio
252	      const processedFiles = await cropImagesToShotAspectRatio(
253	        files,
254	        adjustedShot ?? currentShot,
255	        currentProjectId,
256	        projectsRef.current,
257	        uploadSettingsRef.current
258	      );
259	
260	      // 3. Calculate positions for each file
261	      const positions = processedFiles.map((_, index) =>
262	        calculatedTargetFrame + (index * currentBatchVideoFrames)
263	      );
264	
265	      // 4. Upload with positions (single round trip to database)
266	      const result = await handleExternalImageDropMutationRef.current.mutateAsync({
267	        imageFiles: processedFiles,
268	        targetShotId: currentShot.id,
269	        [REDACTED],
270	        currentShotCount: 0,
271	        skipAutoPosition: false,
272	        positions: positions,
273	      });
274	
275	      if (!result?.generationIds?.length) {
276	        return;
277	      }
278	
279	      // 5. If positions weren't set by the upload mutation, set them now
280	      if (result.generationIds.length > 0) {
281	        const needsPositionUpdate = await (async () => {
282	          const { data } = await supabase().from('shot_generations')
283	            .select('id, timeline_frame')
284	            .eq('shot_id', currentShot.id)
285	            .in('generation_id', result.generationIds)
286	            .limit(1);
287	
288	          return data?.[0]?.timeline_frame === null;
289	        })();
290	
291	        if (needsPositionUpdate) {
292	          await persistTimelinePositions(
293	            currentShot.id,
294	            result.generationIds,
295	            calculatedTargetFrame,
296	            currentBatchVideoFrames
297	          );
298	        }
299	      }
300	
301	      // Demote orphaned video variants now that new images are in place
302	      await demoteOrphanedVariantsRef.current(currentShot.id, 'image-add');
303	
304	    } catch (error) {
305	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'TimelineDrop', toastTitle: 'Failed to add images' });
306	      throw error;
307	    } finally {
308	      actionsRef.current.setUploadingImage(false);
309	    }
310	  }, []);

312	  /**
313	   * Handle dropping a generation from GenerationsPane onto the timeline
314	   */
315	  const handleTimelineGenerationDrop = useCallback(async (
316	    generationId: string,
317	    imageUrl: string,
318	    thumbUrl: string | undefined,
319	    targetFrame?: number
320	  ) => {
321	    const currentShot = selectedShotRef.current;

324	    if (!currentShot?.id || !currentProjectId) {

328

344	      // Demote orphaned video variants now that the new image is in place

348	      throw error;

352	  /**
353	   * Handle dropping external images onto batch mode grid
354	   */
355	  const handleBatchImageDrop = useCallback(async (
356	    files: File[],
357	    targetFrame?: number
358	  ) => {
359	    const currentShot = selectedShotRef.current;
360	    const currentProjectId = projectIdRef.current;
361	    let adjustedShot: Shot | undefined;
362	
363	    if (!currentShot?.id || !currentProjectId) {
364	      toast.error("Cannot add images: No shot or project selected.");
365	      return;
366	    }
367	
368	    // Track optimistic items for cleanup
369	    const optimisticIds: string[] = [];
370	    const localUrls: string[] = [];
371	
372	    try {
373	      try {
374	        const autoAdjustedAspectRatio = await maybeAutoAdjustShotAspectRatio(
375	          files,
376	          currentShot,
377	          projectsRef.current as ProjectWithSettings[],
378	          currentProjectId
379	        );
380	
381	        if (autoAdjustedAspectRatio) {
382	          const ok = await updateShotAspectRatioRef.current(
383	            currentShot.id,
384	            currentProjectId,
385	            autoAdjustedAspectRatio.adjustedTo,
386	            { immediate: true }
387	          );
388	
389	          if (ok) {
390	            setAutoAdjustedAspectRatioRef.current(autoAdjustedAspectRatio);
391	            adjustedShot = {
392	              ...currentShot,
393	              aspect_ratio: autoAdjustedAspectRatio.adjustedTo,
394	            };
395	          } else {
396	            normalizeAndPresentError(
397	              new Error('Failed to auto-adjust shot aspect ratio for the dropped image.'),
398	              {
399	                context: 'DropAutoAdjustShotAspectRatio:update',
400	                showToast: false,
401	                logData: {
402	                  shotId: currentShot.id,
403	                  adjustedTo: autoAdjustedAspectRatio.adjustedTo,
404	                },
405	              }
406	            );
407	          }
408	        }
409	      } catch (error) {
410	        normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
411	          context: 'DropAutoAdjustShotAspectRatio:update',
412	          showToast: false,
413	          logData: { shotId: currentShot.id },
414	        });
415	      }
416	
417	      actionsRef.current.setUploadingImage(true);
418	
419	      // 1. Calculate target frame positions with collision detection
420	      const startFrame = targetFrame ?? await fetchNextAvailableFrameForShot(currentShot.id, undefined);
421	
422	      // For multiple files, ensure each position is unique
423	      const existingGens = queryClientRef.current.getQueryData<GenerationRow[]>(generationQueryKeys.byShot(currentShot.id)) || [];
424	      const existingFrames = existingGens
425	        .filter(g => g.timeline_frame != null && g.timeline_frame !== -1)
426	        .map(g => g.timeline_frame as number);
427	
428	      const positions: number[] = [];
429	      const allUsedFrames = [...existingFrames];
430	      for (let i = 0; i < files.length; i++) {
431	        let targetFrame = startFrame + (i * DEFAULT_FRAME_SPACING);
432	        while (allUsedFrames.includes(targetFrame)) {
433	          targetFrame += 1;
434	        }
435	        positions.push(targetFrame);
436	        allUsedFrames.push(targetFrame);
437	      }
438	
439	      // 2. Create optimistic entries immediately using local file URLs
440	      const previousFastGens = queryClientRef.current.getQueryData<GenerationRow[]>(generationQueryKeys.byShot(currentShot.id)) || [];
441	
442	      const optimisticItems = files.map((file, index) => {
443	        const localUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
444	        localUrls.push(localUrl);
445	        const tempId = `temp-upload-${Date.now()}-${index}-${Math.random()}`;
446	        optimisticIds.push(tempId);
447	
448	        return {
449	          id: tempId,
450	          generation_id: tempId,
451	          shotImageEntryId: tempId,
452	          shot_generation_id: tempId,
453	          location: localUrl,
454	          thumbnail_url: localUrl,
455	          imageUrl: localUrl,
456	          thumbUrl: localUrl,
457	          timeline_frame: positions[index],
458	          type: 'image' as const,
459	          created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
460	          starred: false,
461	          name: file.name,
462	          based_on: null,
463	          params: {},
464	          shot_data: { [currentShot.id]: [positions[index]] },
465	          _optimistic: true,
466	          _uploading: true
467	        };
468	      });
469	
470	      // Add optimistic items to cache
471	      queryClientRef.current.setQueryData(
472	        generationQueryKeys.byShot(currentShot.id),
473	        [...previousFastGens, ...optimisticItems]
474	      );
475	
476	      // 3. Crop images
477	      const processedFiles = await cropImagesToShotAspectRatio(
478	        files,
479	        adjustedShot ?? currentShot,
480	        currentProjectId,
481	        projectsRef.current,
482	        uploadSettingsRef.current
483	      );
484	
485	      // 4. Upload with positions
486	      const result = await handleExternalImageDropMutationRef.current.mutateAsync({
487	        imageFiles: processedFiles,
488	        targetShotId: currentShot.id,
489	        [REDACTED],
490	        currentShotCount: 0,
491	        skipAutoPosition: false,
492	        positions: positions,
493	      });
494	
495	      if (!result?.generationIds?.length) {
496	        return;
497	      }
498	
499	      // 5. If positions weren't set by the upload, set them now (fallback)
500	      const { data: checkData } = await supabase().from('shot_generations')
501	        .select('id, timeline_frame')
502	        .eq('shot_id', currentShot.id)
503	        .in('generation_id', result.generationIds)
504	        .limit(1);
505	
506	      if (checkData?.[0]?.timeline_frame === null) {
507	        await persistTimelinePositions(
508	          currentShot.id,
509	          result.generationIds,
510	          startFrame,
511	          1 // Use 1 frame spacing for batch mode
512	        );
513	      }
514	
515	      // Demote orphaned video variants now that new images are in place
516	      await demoteOrphanedVariantsRef.current(currentShot.id, 'image-add');
517	
518	    } catch (error) {
519	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'BatchDrop', toastTitle: 'Failed to add images' });
520	
521	      // Remove optimistic items on error
522	      const currentCache = queryClientRef.current.getQueryData<GenerationRow[]>(generationQueryKeys.byShot(currentShot.id)) || [];
523	      queryClientRef.current.setQueryData(
524	        generationQueryKeys.byShot(currentShot.id),
525	        currentCache.filter(item => !optimisticIds.includes(item.id))
526	      );
527	
528	      throw error;
529	    } finally {
530	      actionsRef.current.setUploadingImage(false);
531	
532	      // Clean up local URLs to prevent memory leaks
533	      localUrls.forEach(url => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));
534	    }
535	  }, []);
536

540	  const handleBatchGenerationDrop = useCallback(async (

577	  const handleVariantDrop = useCallback(async (params: VariantDropParams) => {
578	    const currentShot = selectedShotRef.current;
579	    const currentProjectId = projectIdRef.current;
580	
581	    if (!currentShot?.id || !currentProjectId) {

583	      return;
584	    }
585	
586	    try {
587	      if (params.sourceGenerationId) {
588	        if (!params.imageUrl) {
589	          toast.error("Cannot add variant: Missing source image.");
590	          return;
591	        }
592	
593	        const { error } = await supabase().from('generation_variants').insert({
594	          generation_id: params.targetGenerationId,
595	          project_id: currentProjectId,
596	          location: params.imageUrl,
597	          thumbnail_url: params.thumbUrl || params.imageUrl,
598	          is_primary: params.mode === 'main',
599	          variant_type: VARIANT_TYPE.DROPPED,
600	          params: {
601	            source: 'generation-drop',
602	            source_generation_id: params.sourceGenerationId,
603	            source_variant_id: params.sourceVariantId || null,
604	          },
605	        });
606	
607	        if (error) {
608	          throw error;
609	        }
610	      } else if (params.files?.length) {
611	        const [file] = params.files;
612	        if (!file) {
613	          return;
614	        }
615	
616	        const { imageUrl, thumbnailUrl } = await uploadImageForVariant(file, currentProjectId);
617	        const { error } = await supabase().from('generation_variants').insert({
618	          generation_id: params.targetGenerationId,
619	          project_id: currentProjectId,
620	          location: imageUrl,
621	          thumbnail_url: thumbnailUrl || imageUrl,
622	          is_primary: params.mode === 'main',
623	          variant_type: VARIANT_TYPE.DROPPED,
624	          params: {
625	            source: 'file-drop',
626	            original_filename: file.name,
627	          },
628	        });
629	
630	        if (error) {
631	          throw error;
632	        }
633	      } else {
634	        return;
635	      }
636	
637	      await enqueueVariantInvalidation(queryClientRef.current, {
638	        generationId: params.targetGenerationId,
639	        shotId: currentShot.id,
640	        reason: 'variant-drop',
641	      });
642	
643	      if (params.mode === 'main') {
644	        await queryClientRef.current.invalidateQueries({
645	          [REDACTED](currentShot.id),
646	        });
647	      }
648	    } catch (error) {
649	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'VariantDrop', toastTitle: 'Failed to add variant' });
650	      throw error;
651	    }
652	  }, []);
653	
654	  return {
655	    handleTimelineImageDrop,
656	    handleTimelineGenerationDrop,
657	    handleBatchImageDrop,
658	    handleBatchGenerationDrop,
659	    handleVariantDrop,
660	  };
661	};

109	  const userId = sessionResult.data.session?.user?.id;
110	  if (!userId) {
111	    throw new Error('User not authenticated');
112	  }
113	
114	  const { data, error } = await client
115	    .from('local_media_handles')
116	    .insert({
117	      user_id: userId,
118	      project_id: projectId,
119	    })
120	    .select('id')
121	    .single();
122	
123	  if (error || !data?.id) {
124	    throw error instanceof Error ? error : new Error('Failed to create local media handle row');
125	  }
126	
127	  return data.id;
128	}
129	
130	async function insertLocalGeneration(input: {
131	  file: File;
132	  projectId: string;
133	  mediaType: 'image' | 'video';
134	  thumbnailUrl: string;
135	  localHandleId: string;
136	}): Promise<GenerationRow> {
137	  const client = getSupabaseClient() as unknown as {
138	    from: (table: string) => {
139	      insert: (payload: Record<string, unknown>) => {
140	        select: () => {
141	          single: () => Promise<{ data: GenerationRow | null; error: unknown }>;
142	        };
143	      };
144	    };
145	  };
146	
147	  const generationParams: GenerationParams = {
148	    extra: {
149	      source: 'upload',
150	      original_filename: input.file.name,
151	      file_type: input.file.type,
152	      file_size: input.file.size,
153	    },
154	  };
155	
156	  const { data, error } = await client
157	    .from('generations')
158	    .insert({
159	      location: null,
160	      thumbnail_url: input.thumbnailUrl,
161	      type: input.mediaType,
162	      project_id: input.projectId,
163	      params: generationParams,
164	      storage_mode: 'local',
165	      local_handle_id: input.localHandleId,
166	      local_file_name: input.file.name,
167	      local_file_size: input.file.size,
168	      local_file_mime: input.file.type,
169	    })
170	    .select()
171	    .single();
172	
173	  if (error || !data) {
174	    throw error instanceof Error ? error : new Error('Failed to create local generation');
175	  }
176	
177	  return data;
178	}
179	
180	export async function createGenerationForLocalFile(
181	  input: CreateLocalGenerationInput,
182	): Promise<GenerationRow> {
183	  const thumbnailUrl = await uploadThumbnailOnly({
184	    file: input.file,
185	    mediaType: input.mediaType,
186	  });
187	  const localHandleId = await insertLocalMediaHandle(input.projectId);
188	  await saveHandle(localHandleId, input.handle);
189	
190	  return insertLocalGeneration({
191	    file: input.file,
192	    projectId: input.projectId,
193	    mediaType: input.mediaType,
194	    thumbnailUrl,
195	    localHandleId,
196	  });
197	}
198	
199	export async function createGenerationForUploadedImage(
200	  input: CreateGenerationForUploadedImageInput,
201	): Promise<GenerationRow> {
202	  const { imageFile, projectId, onProgress } = input;
203	  const { imageUrl, thumbnailUrl } = await uploadImageForVariant(imageFile, projectId, {
204	    onProgress,
205	  });
206	
207	  const generationParams: GenerationParams = {
208	    source: 'upload',
209	    original_filename: imageFile.name,
210	    file_type: imageFile.type,
211	    file_size: imageFile.size,
212	  };
213	
214	  return insertUploadedGeneration({
215	    projectId,
216	    type: 'image',
217	    location: imageUrl,
218	    thumbnailUrl: thumbnailUrl || imageUrl,
219	    generationParams,
220	  });
221	}
222	
223	async function uploadVideoPosterFrame(videoFile: File): Promise<string> {
224	  const posterBlob = await extractVideoPosterFrame(videoFile);
225	  return uploadBlobToStorage(posterBlob, `${videoFile.name}-poster.jpg`, 'image/jpeg');
226	}
227	
228	export async function createGenerationForUploadedVideo(
229	  input: CreateGenerationForUploadedVideoInput,
230	): Promise<GenerationRow> {
231	  const { videoFile, projectId, onProgress } = input;
232	  const videoUrl = await uploadVideoToStorage(videoFile, { onProgress });
233	
234	  let thumbnailUrl = videoUrl;
235	  try {
236	    thumbnailUrl = await uploadVideoPosterFrame(videoFile);
237	  } catch (error) {
238	    normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
239	      context: `createGenerationForUploadedVideo:thumbnail:${videoFile.name}`,
240	      showToast: false,
241	    });
242	  }
243	
244	  const generationParams: GenerationParams = {
245	    source: 'upload',
246	    original_filename: videoFile.name,
247	    file_type: videoFile.type,
248	    file_size: videoFile.size,
249	  };
250	
251	  return insertUploadedGeneration({
252	    projectId,
253	    type: 'video',
254	    location: videoUrl,
255	    thumbnailUrl,
256	    generationParams,
257	  });
258	}
259

126	export function useShotSettingsValue({
127	  // Core
128	  selectedShot,
129	  selectedShotId,

234	      ) => {

239	          targetFrame

243	        await generationActions.handleVariantDrop(params);
244	      },

246	        generationActions.handleDeleteImageFromShot(id);
247	      },
248	      onBatchDelete: (ids: string[]) => {

253	      },

255	    }),
256	    [generationActions, handleImageReorder, handleImageUpload]

258	
259	  // Build shot management domain for context

269	    }),

272	
273	  // Build final context value

1	import { toast } from '@/shared/components/ui/runtime/sonner';
2	
3	const VALID_IMAGE_TYPES = ['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp', 'image/jpg'];
4	
5	export function filterValidTimelineImageFiles(files: File[]): File[] {
6	  return files.filter((file) => {
7	    if (VALID_IMAGE_TYPES.includes(file.type)) {
8	      return true;
9	    }
10	    toast.error(`Invalid file type for ${file.name}. Only JPEG, PNG, and WebP are supported.`);
11	    return false;
12	  });
13	}

1	/**
2	 * useEmptyStateDrop - drag-and-drop handling for the empty timeline state.
3	 *
4	 * Supports both file drops and internal generation drops.
5	 * Returns drag state and handlers for the empty-state overlay.
6	 */
7	
8	import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
9	import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
10	import { filterValidTimelineImageFiles } from './imageDropValidation';
11	
12	interface UseEmptyStateDropProps {
13	  onFileDrop?: (files: File[], targetFrame?: number) => Promise<void>;
14	  onGenerationDrop?: (generationId: string, imageUrl: string, thumbUrl: string | undefined, targetFrame?: number) => Promise<void>;
15	  onImageUpload?: (files: File[]) => Promise<void>;
16	}
17	
18	interface UseEmptyStateDropReturn {
19	  isDragOver: boolean;
20	  dragType: 'file' | 'generation' | null;
21	  handleEmptyStateDragEnter: (e: React.DragEvent) => void;
22	  handleEmptyStateDragOver: (e: React.DragEvent) => void;
23	  handleEmptyStateDragLeave: (e: React.DragEvent) => void;
24	  handleEmptyStateDrop: (e: React.DragEvent) => Promise<void>;
25	}
26	
27	export function useEmptyStateDrop({
28	  onFileDrop,
29	  onGenerationDrop,
30	  onImageUpload,
31	}: UseEmptyStateDropProps): UseEmptyStateDropReturn {
32	  const [isDragOver, setIsDragOver] = useState(false);
33	  const [dragType, setDragType] = useState<'file' | 'generation' | null>(null);
34	
35	  const handleEmptyStateDragEnter = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
36	    e.preventDefault();
37	    e.stopPropagation();
38	
39	    if (e.dataTransfer.types.includes('application/x-generation') && onGenerationDrop) {
40	      setIsDragOver(true);
41	      setDragType('generation');
42	    } else if (e.dataTransfer.types.includes('Files') && onFileDrop) {
43	      setIsDragOver(true);
44	      setDragType('file');
45	    }
46	  }, [onFileDrop, onGenerationDrop]);
47	
48	  const handleEmptyStateDragOver = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
49	    e.preventDefault();
50	    e.stopPropagation();
51	
52	    if (e.dataTransfer.types.includes('application/x-generation') && onGenerationDrop) {
53	      setIsDragOver(true);
54	      setDragType('generation');
55	      e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy';
56	    } else if (e.dataTransfer.types.includes('Files') && onFileDrop) {
57	      setIsDragOver(true);
58	      setDragType('file');
59	      e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy';
60	    } else {
61	      e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'none';
62	    }
63	  }, [onFileDrop, onGenerationDrop]);
64	
65	  const handleEmptyStateDragLeave = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
66	    e.preventDefault();
67	    e.stopPropagation();
68	    const rect = (e.currentTarget as HTMLElement).getBoundingClientRect();
69	    const x = e.clientX;
70	    const y = e.clientY;
71	    if (x < rect.left || x >= rect.right || y < rect.top || y >= rect.bottom) {
72	      setIsDragOver(false);
73	      setDragType(null);
74	    }
75	  }, []);
76	
77	  const handleEmptyStateDrop = useCallback(async (e: React.DragEvent) => {
78	    e.preventDefault();
79	    e.stopPropagation();
80	    setIsDragOver(false);
81	    setDragType(null);
82	
83	    // Handle internal generation drop first
84	    if (e.dataTransfer.types.includes('application/x-generation') && onGenerationDrop) {
85	      try {
86	        const dataString = e.dataTransfer.getData('application/x-generation');
87	        if (dataString) {
88	          const data = JSON.parse(dataString);
89	          if (data.generationId && data.imageUrl) {
90	            await onGenerationDrop(data.generationId, data.imageUrl, data.thumbUrl, 0);
91	            return;
92	          }
93	        }
94	      } catch (error) {
95	        normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'Timeline', toastTitle: 'Failed to add image' });
96	      }
97	      return;
98	    }
99	
100	    // Handle file drop
101	    if (!onImageUpload) return;
102	
103	    const files = Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files);
104	    if (files.length === 0) return;
105	
106	    const validFiles = filterValidTimelineImageFiles(files);
107	
108	    if (validFiles.length === 0) return;
109	
110	    try {
111	      await onImageUpload(validFiles);
112	    } catch (error) {
113	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'Timeline', toastTitle: 'Failed to add images' });
114	    }
115	  }, [onImageUpload, onGenerationDrop]);
116	
117	  return {
118	    isDragOver,
119	    dragType,
120	    handleEmptyStateDragEnter,
121	    handleEmptyStateDragOver,
122	    handleEmptyStateDragLeave,
123	    handleEmptyStateDrop,
124	  };
125	}
126
```

45	// ============================================================================
46	// Image Processing
47	// ============================================================================
48	
49	/**
50	 * Crop images to match the shot's aspect ratio
51	 * @returns Processed files (cropped or original if cropping fails/disabled)
52	 */
53	export const cropImagesToShotAspectRatio = async (
54	  files: File[],
55	  selectedShot: Shot | undefined,
56	  projectId: string,
57	  projects: Project[],
58	  uploadSettings: UploadSettings | undefined
59	): Promise<File[]> => {
60	  // Skip cropping if disabled
61	  if (uploadSettings?.cropToProjectSize === false) {
62	    return files;
63	  }
64	
65	  // Determine aspect ratio (prioritize shot over project)
66	  const currentProject = projects.find(p => p.id === projectId);
67	  const aspectRatioStr = selectedShot?.aspect_ratio || 
68	                        currentProject?.aspectRatio || 
69	                        currentProject?.settings?.aspectRatio;
70	  
71	  if (!aspectRatioStr) {
72	    return files;
73	  }
74	
75	  const targetAspectRatio = parseRatio(aspectRatioStr);
76	  
77	  if (isNaN(targetAspectRatio)) {
78	    return files;
79	  }
80	
81	  // Crop each file
82	  const cropPromises = files.map(async (file) => {
83	    try {
84	      const result = await cropImageToProjectAspectRatio(file, targetAspectRatio);
85	      if (result) {
86	        return result.croppedFile;
87	      }
88	      return file; // Return original if cropping fails
89	    } catch (error) {
90	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'ImageCrop', showToast: false });
91	      return file; // Return original on error
92	    }
93	  });
94	
95	  return await Promise.all(cropPromises);
96	};
97

68	
69	  return useMutation({
70	    mutationFn: async (variables: ExternalImageDropVariables) => {
71	      const { currentProjectQueryKey } = variables;
72	      if (!currentProjectQueryKey) {
73	        toast.error('Cannot add image(s): current project is not identified.');
74	        return null;
75	      }
76	
77	      try {
78	        return await processDroppedImages({
79	          variables,

81	          createShot: createShotMutation.mutateAsync,

87	        return null;
88	      }
89	    },
90	  });
91	};

The plan only threads handles through `useUnifiedDrop` → `handleTimelineImageDrop`, so a user dropping a >25 MB image on an empty timeline still hits the upload path with no chance to capture a handle. The brief's stated goal is 'when a user drops a large file onto the timeline', which the empty-state drop also satisfies; the plan should either thread handles through `useEmptyStateDrop` → `handleBatchImageDrop` as well, or explicitly mark this as out of scope so the executor doesn't ship a half-fixed feature.\",\n      \"category\": \"completeness\",\n      \"severity_hint\": \"likely-minor\",\n      \"evidence\": \"src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/hooks/drag/useEmptyStateDrop.ts:77-114 (file drop calls onImageUpload, not onFileDrop); src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/controllers/useImageManagementController.ts:53-57 (handleImageUpload → handleBatchImageDrop); plan Step 2 enumerates useEmptyStateDrop.ts:13 only for the optional onFileDrop signature mirror, not for actually capturing handles.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"FLAG-002\",\n      \"concern\": \"`externalImageDrop.test.ts` mock strategy is under-specified. Step 5.1's instruction to 'mock `createGenerationForLocalFile` (mocked module `@/shared/lib/media/createGenerationFromFile`)' would, if implemented as a full module mock, also replace `createGenerationForUploadedImage` and break the existing 'uploads, creates generation, and attaches with explicit timeline position' case. The existing test deliberately leaves `@/shared/lib/media/createGenerationFromFile` unmocked so the default `createGeneration = createGenerationForUploadedImage` path runs end-to-end against the mocked `clientThumbnailGenerator` and `imageUploader`, and asserts on `mocks.uploadImageWithThumbnail`. The plan should specify either a partial mock via `vi.importActual` or mocking the local-path's leaf deps (`saveHandle`, `uploadBlobToStorage`, `extractVideoPosterFrame`, plus the supabase `from('local_media_handles')`/`from('generations')` inserts already in the test harness) so the existing upload-path assertions still hold.\",\n      \"category\": \"verifiability\",\n      \"severity_hint\": \"likely-minor\",\n      \"evidence\": \"src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.test.ts:28-35,73-100,123-177 — mocks clientThumbnailGenerator and imageUploader and asserts on mocks.uploadImageWithThumbnail/mocks.uploadImageToStorage; plan Step 5.1 says to mock createGenerationForLocalFile via the createGenerationFromFile module without addressing the side effect on createGenerationForUploadedImage.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"verified_flag_ids\": [],\n  \"disputed_flag_ids\": []\n}"

> AGENT

Plan looks fundamentally sound — synchronous handle capture, optional plumbing, crop reference-equality re-alignment, and caller audit are all addressed correctly. Two minor concerns flagged: (1) empty-state drop path (`useEmptyStateDrop` → `handleBatchImageDrop`) isn't wired for handle capture, so large drops on an empty timeline still upload; (2) the proposed test mock for `createGenerationFromFile` would inadvertently break existing `externalImageDrop.test.ts` assertions about `uploadImageWithThumbnail` unless it uses partial mocking.

Note: file writes to the critique_output.json target were rejected by the permission/sandbox layer despite repeated retries via Write, Edit, Bash heredoc/printf redirection, and Python; findings were captured via StructuredOutput as the final-turn channel.
